local __mh_ok, __mh_input = pcall(function()
  return (getgenv and getgenv().MEENHUB_KEY) or _G.MEENHUB_KEY
end)

  return __mh_payload(...)
end

local __mh_plr = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local __mh_gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
__mh_gui.Name = "MEENHUB_KeyGate"
__mh_gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
__mh_gui.Parent = __mh_plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local __mh_box = Instance.new("Frame")
__mh_box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 150)
__mh_box.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -75)
__mh_box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
__mh_box.BorderSizePixel = 0
__mh_box.Parent = __mh_gui
Instance.new("UICorner", __mh_box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

local __mh_title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
__mh_title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 30)
__mh_title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
__mh_title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
__mh_title.Text = "MEEN HUB — ใส่คีย์เพื่อเข้าใช้งาน"
__mh_title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245)
__mh_title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
__mh_title.TextSize = 14
__mh_title.Parent = __mh_box

local __mh_input_box = Instance.new("TextBox")
__mh_input_box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 36)
__mh_input_box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 55)
__mh_input_box.PlaceholderText = "วางคีย์ที่นี่..."
__mh_input_box.Text = ""
__mh_input_box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 36)

local __mh_submit = Instance.new("TextButton")

local __mh_done = false
local function __mh_try()
  if __mh_done then return end
  if __mh_input_box.Text == __mh_key then
    __mh_done = true
    __mh_gui:Destroy()
    __mh_payload()
  else
    __mh_title.Text = "คีย์ไม่ถูกต้อง ลองใหม่อีกครั้ง"
    __mh_title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(239, 63, 102)
  end
end
__mh_submit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(__mh_try)
__mh_input_box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
  if enterPressed then __mh_try() end
end)
